- **chinglish**: Transform Chinese text into Chinglish (中式英語) — English that is heavily influenced by Chinese grammar, word order, and thought patterns, producing the characteristic "中式英文" style found on signs, menus, instructions, and everyday speech. Can also take English text and re-render it through a Chinese-thinking lens to produce Chinglish. Applies a 25-item checklist covering article errors, copula dropping, topic-comment structure, verb confusion, literal calques, tense flattening, and more. Useful for humor, creative writing, language education, or demonstrating L1 transfer patterns. Triggers on "/… ([tzengyuxio/skills](https://github.com/tzengyuxio/skills/tree/HEAD/chinglish) · ★46)
- **chinglish-collect**: Mine Chinese documents and their existing English translations for Chinglish case fragments, filing each into a cases folder as a source plus bad-English pair. - **chip-seq**: ChIP-seq peak calling and downstream interpretation with MACS3, signal track export, annotation, motif analysis, and differential binding review. ([Runchuan-BU/BioClaw](https://github.com/Runchuan-BU/BioClaw/tree/HEAD/container/skills/chip-seq) · ★411 · 4 copies)
- **chip-seq-analysis**: ChIP-seq analysis — peak calling, differential binding, motif discovery, and QC with phantom-peak metrics. ([aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills](https://github.com/aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chip-seq-analysis) · ★68 · 3 copies)
- **chip-seq-anchor-occupancy-overlap-validation**: Use when a set of chromatin loop calls (e.g., bedpe from MICC/ChIA-PET2) needs orthogonal validation against independent CTCF/cohesin ChIP-seq peaks in the same cell line (e.g., GM12878). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-anchor-occupancy-overlap-validation))
- **chip-seq-binding-library-based-tr-identification**: Use when a set of differentially accessible region BED files (e.g., from bulk TR perturbation cKO experiments via DiffBind export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-binding-library-based-tr-identification))
- **chip-seq-broad-domain-calling**: Use when the goal is to identify broad ChIP-seq enrichment domains spanning kb to Mb scales (e.g., H3K27me3 or H3K9me3) from mapped-read BED files, especially where whole-domain integrity (covering full gene bodies of repressed genes) matters more than sharp peak positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-broad-domain-calling))
- **chip-seq-coverage-binning**: Use when raw ChIP-seq BAM files for one or more epigenomic marks (e.g. ENCODE K562 H3K4me3/H3K9ac) plus a MACS/MACS2 peak list must be turned into fixed-width binned coverage profiles for shape-based alignment or clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-coverage-binning))
- **chip-seq-coverage-profile-generation**: Use when mapped ChIP-Seq short reads (BED or ELAND format, or paired-end with known insert size) must be converted into a binned, end-weighted WIG-format coverage profile as input to HMM-based peak calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-coverage-profile-generation))
- **chip-seq-data-retrieval-from-geo**: Use when a differential ChIP-seq analysis needs its raw aligned reads (BAM) fetched from public repositories, specifically when reproducing the ENCODE H1-hESC vs K562 histone mark comparison via GEO sample accessions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-data-retrieval-from-geo))
- **chip-seq-duplicate-read-removal**: Use when aligned ChIP-seq BAM files are about to be binned and counted (e.g. with histoneHMM getBins/countBamInGRanges) and PCR/sequencing duplicates would inflate per-bin read counts; the article applied this to all H3K27me3/H3K9me3 datasets from rat, mouse and human cell lines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-duplicate-read-removal))
- **chip-seq-enriched-vs-background-binning**: Use when ChIP-Seq short-read data (e.g. BED/ELAND single-end or pair-end) must be binned into enriched vs. background regions to model read-count distributions or call protein-DNA binding peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-enriched-vs-background-binning))
- **chip-seq-genome-binning-and-hdf-counting**: Use when raw aligned ChIP-Seq reads (BED or ELAND format, single- or pair-end) must be converted into a fixed-bin coverage profile prior to peak calling or HMM classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-genome-binning-and-hdf-counting))
- **chip-seq-genome-browser-profile-generation**: Use when you have BAM/SAM files of mapped ChIP-seq reads and need genome-wide coverage profiles (BedGraph) for genome-browser visualization or for testing sequencing-depth saturation of a histone mark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-genome-browser-profile-generation))
- **chip-seq-gold-standard-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether predicted chromatin accessibility (e.g., BIRD-predicted DH) can rank TF motif sites correctly, by comparing against ENCODE ChIP-seq peaks for known TFs such as ELF1 in GM12878. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-gold-standard-benchmarking))
- **chip-seq-ground-truth-network-construction**: Use when benchmarking GRN inference methods (scPRINT, scGPT, DeepSEM, Geneformer, GENIE3) against cell type-specific ChIP-seq or perturb-seq ground truths such as MCalla et al. ESC networks or K562 gwps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-ground-truth-network-construction))
- **chip-seq-ground-truth-network-evaluation**: Use when a ranked TF–gene edge list has been produced from scRNA-seq data (e.g. Tabula Muris tissue data) and needs quantitative validation against ChIP-Atlas-derived ground-truth GRNs per cell type using AUROC and EPR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-ground-truth-network-evaluation))
- **chip-seq-histone-modification-data-processing**: Use when raw ChIP-Seq BAM files for histone marks (e.g. wgEncodeBroadHistoneK562H3k4me3StdAlnRep1.bam) must be converted into binned enrichment profiles over peak regions before alignment/clustering with DGW. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-histone-modification-data-processing))
- **chip-seq-motif-prediction**: Use when raw or processed human ChIP-seq peak calls (e.g. ENCODE BED with summits) are available and the goal is to discover TF-DNA binding motifs with significance scores and compare them to documented motifs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-motif-prediction))
- **chip-seq-peak-calling**: Use when a ChIP-seq FASTQ dataset (with a matched input/control) needs peaks, peak annotation, or motif identification, and CIPHER is being used to run the full two-stage pipeline from raw reads to called peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-peak-calling--9572d2e9701b16d4))
- **chip-seq-peak-calling**: Use when the input is raw ChIP-Seq short reads (BED or ELAND format, human or mouse) or BED files for transcription-factor or histone-mark datasets, and the goal is to identify read-enriched regions and compare peak callers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-peak-calling--8607454362b9598f))
- **chip-seq-peak-calling-for-loop-anchors**: Use when processing ChIA-PET data where chromatin loops must be called between ChIP-Seq-like peak anchors, e.g. after bwa alignment and removeDup, before bedpe2Interaction/MICC loop calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-peak-calling-for-loop-anchors))
- **chip-seq-peak-calling-replicate-handling**: Use when raw sequencing data of lncRNA:DNA capture experiments (ChIRP-seq, ChOP-seq, CHART-seq, RAP-seq) from GEO/ENA must be uniformly re-processed and replicate-inconsistent peaks reconciled into benchmark BED peak sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-peak-calling-replicate-handling))
- **chip-seq-peak-calling-with-control**: Use when starting from aligned-read BAM files of one or more ChIP-seq marks plus a matched input/control BAM, and you need enriched peak regions (e.g. as BED input for unsupervised alignment/clustering) rather than gene-anchored windows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-peak-calling-with-control))
- **chip-seq-peak-calling-workflow**: Use when when you have aligned ChIP-Seq reads (single-end BED or paired-end BEDPE format) and need to identify enriched genomic regions by comparing ChIP signal against control background, with the ability to customize fragment length estimation, local bias calculation, and peak score thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/chip-seq-peak-calling-workflow))
- **chip-seq-peak-motif-classification**: Use when ChIP-seq peaks for multiple TFs (e.g. the 100 TFs expressed in K562) have DESSO-predicted motifs and TFBSs, and you need to separate direct from indirect binding to infer tethering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-peak-motif-classification))
- **chip-seq-peak-overlap-annotation**: Use when peak-gene pairs (e.g., GrID-Net vs correlation baselines on 10x Multiome) must be validated for enhancer priming states, requiring H3K4me1/H3K27ac ChIP-seq overlap of ATAC peak coordinates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-peak-overlap-annotation))
- **chip-seq-peak-sequence-extraction**: Use when preparing summit-centered, score-annotated FASTA inputs for MEPP positional motif profiling from MACS2 narrowpeak calls (e.g. ENCODE K562 GATA1 ChIP-seq or mESC Nanog ChIP-seq/ChIP-nexus peaks). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-peak-sequence-extraction))
- **chip-seq-peak-signal-integration**: Use when you have TR ChIP-seq peak bed files with signal enrichment values and gene TSS annotations and need quantitative TR-target scores (RP scores) rather than binary target lists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-peak-signal-integration))
- **chip-seq-peak-strand-orientation-analysis**: Use when strand-annotated ChIP-seq peak BED files and strand-assigned TFBS BED files are available and the goal is to test whether motif-level strand asymmetry (e.g. CTCF non-template strand preference) is recapitulated within peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-peak-strand-orientation-analysis))
- **chip-seq-phasing-track-integration**: Use when computing A/B compartment eigenvectors from Hi-C cool files whose sign must be anchored to an external chromatin mark, e.g. phasing cooltools E1 with the ENCODE H3K4me3 (ENCFF818GNV) track for GM12878. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/chip-seq-phasing-track-integration))
- **chip-seq-quality-atlas-stratification**: Use when compiling ChiLin QC outputs (e.g., id_frip FRiP JSON records) and you need to benchmark what proportion of ChIP versus input samples exceed a 1% FRiP threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-quality-atlas-stratification))
- **chip-seq-read-alignment-filtering**: Use when when beginning peak calling on ChIP-Seq data: you have raw single-end or paired-end BED/BEDPE alignment files for both ChIP and control samples and need to remove duplicate reads before predicting fragment length and building local bias models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/chip-seq-read-alignment-filtering))
- **chip-seq-read-alignment-spike-in-genome**: Use when raw ChIP-seq FASTQ libraries from a spike-in experiment (e.g. Jurkat H3K79me2 GSE60104 with fruit fly spike-in) must be mapped to a concatenated synthetic reference and filtered before genome segmentation and normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-read-alignment-spike-in-genome))
- **chip-seq-read-count-binning**: Use when preparing raw ChIP-seq BAM files of broad-footprint histone modifications (e.g. H3K27me3) for histoneHMM differential or single-sample calling, where per-bin bivariate read counts must be computed before HMM fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-read-count-binning))
- **chip-seq-read-quality-trimming**: Use when raw Illumina ChIP-seq FASTQ files (e.g., TF immunoprecipitation samples from GEO accession GSE136050) must be adapter- and quality-trimmed prior to genome alignment and macs2 peak calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-read-quality-trimming))
- **chip-seq-read-window-binning**: Use when preparing mapped ChIP-seq (or control) BED files for broad-domain calling with RECOGNICER, i.e. when the analysis requires converting raw read coordinates into nucleosome-resolution (default 200 bp) binary blocks before recursive block transformation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-read-window-binning))
- **chip-seq-regulatory-potential-annotation**: Use when a data-learned gene program (e.g., one of the 86 MeCs) needs candidate transcriptional regulators, and TF ChIP-seq binding potentials (Lisa) can be combined with per-gene program weights. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-regulatory-potential-annotation))
- **chip-seq-sequence-encoding-and-gc-matched-background-sampling**: Use when predicting TF-DNA binding specificity from ENCODE ChIP-seq peaks with long DNA sequences (1001 bp) and a gated CNN, or when comparing GCNN AUCs against the 101-bp one-layer CNN baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-sequence-encoding-and-gc-matched-background-sampling))
- **chip-seq-signal-aggregation-over-peaks**: Use when benchmarking peak callers on qbed calling-cards data and you need orthogonal validation: average ChIP-seq (bigWig) signal under each caller's peaks plus pairwise peak-overlap concordance between callers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-signal-aggregation-over-peaks))
- **chip-seq-signal-benchmarking**: Use when validating Hi-C-derived sub-compartment probabilities against independent enrichment tracks (TSA-seq, ChIP-seq, cTSA-seq) and comparing correlation performance against published compartment callers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-signal-benchmarking))
- **chip-seq-signal-pileup-extension**: Use when after duplicate filtering and fragment length prediction (d) in ChIP-Seq analysis, when you need to convert discrete read alignments into continuous coverage signal for comparison against control background. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/chip-seq-signal-pileup-extension))
- **chip-seq-signal-scoring-of-motif-sites**: Use when motif-centered genomic sequences need a continuous biological-activity score (e.g. mESC Nanog ChIP-seq signal from GSE203135) so MEPP can profile partner-motif enrichment along a score continuum instead of arbitrary binding thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/chip-seq-signal-scoring-of-motif-sites))

Use when a task requires compiled pair-energy kernels, periodic boundaries, deterministic multi-seed simulations, RDF accumulation, energy/error estimates, electrostatic decomposition, runtime forecasting, or exact Harbor output files without quadratic Python post-processing. ([Osgood001/playground-s4-skills](https://github.com/Osgood001/playground-s4-skills/tree/HEAD/archive/chloroform-community-v2) · 2 copies)
- **chloroform-mc-gated**: Build and run the S4 liquid-chloroform Stockmayer Monte Carlo task through deterministic compile, RNG, move-coverage, energy-drift, provenance, and output gates. Use when implementing or repairing the compiled 81-mobile plus 42-boundary simulation, especially after stale-binary runs, invalid random-number scaling, biased move selection, low acceptance, unbounded RDF work, or unsupported scored outputs. ([Osgood001/playground-s4-skills](https://github.com/Osgood001/playground-s4-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chloroform-mc-gated))
- **chm-html-modernisation**: 《罗德岛行动手册》CHM 项目通用 HTML 改版基准。把旧写法页面（div#content、内联 STYLE、custom-table、h4/h5/h6 标题、p.light 卡片）改成项目现行的“修改版写法”。适用于本手册任意章节的页面，不限于职业页。 ([Astratide/ROH-online](https://github.com/Astratide/ROH-online/tree/HEAD/topics/.agents/skills/chm-html-modernisation))
- **chm13-t2t-assembly-insertion-extraction**: Use when validating novel-sequence-insertion calls against a haploid genome truth set, specifically when CHM13 synthetic long-read callsets need to be confirmed against the T2T-CHM13 assembly (target ~78% confirmation rate). - **chmod-group**: Manage directory group permissions for shared access. Two modes - recursive (files+dirs) and path-only (directory traversal). Useful for sharing checkpoints, data directories with team members. ([KangOxford/auto-quant-research](https://github.com/KangOxford/auto-quant-research/tree/HEAD/execution-layer/skills/chmod-group))
- **chmod-world**: Set up a world-accessible shared folder on HPC Lustre where anyone (including cross-group) can read/write, and newly created files inherit permissive perms. Handles the common HPC reality that setfacl/cron/systemd-linger are locked down. Complements chmod-group (which only covers in-group sharing). ([KangOxford/auto-quant-research](https://github.com/KangOxford/auto-quant-research/tree/HEAD/execution-layer/skills/chmod-world))

- **chon-phan-mem-cong-cu**: Chọn phần mềm, công cụ, nền tảng A.I cho doanh nghiệp theo việc cần làm chứ không theo thương hiệu: đối chiếu phần mềm đang có để tránh mua chồng chéo, điều kiện bắt buộc trước chấm điểm, bằng chứng chính thức có ngày, tổng chi phí sở hữu, dùng thử và phương án rút lui; rà soát khi đến kỳ gia hạn. Dùng khi nghe "nên dùng phần mềm nào", "so sánh mấy công cụ này", "có nên mua", "gia hạn hay bỏ", "phần mềm chồng chéo". Không thuộc Skill này: mua, ký hợp đồng, tạo tài khoản, triển khai, quản lý tài khoản và bản quyền. ([dangtuabm/abm-digital-workforce-sme](https://github.com/dangtuabm/abm-digital-workforce-sme/tree/HEAD/skills/chon-phan-mem-cong-cu))
- **chon-uu-dai-thue-fta**: So thuế nhập MFN với thuế ưu đãi FTA (ATIGA, ACFTA, RCEP, CPTPP, EVFTA...) cho một mặt hàng, chỉ ra mẫu C/O và điều kiện cần đáp ứng. Dùng khi nói "dùng form nào", "ưu đãi FTA". ([mochistudio-vn/kitviet](https://github.com/mochistudio-vn/kitviet/tree/HEAD/xuat-nhap-khau/claude-code/kv/skills/chon-uu-dai-thue-fta) · ★22)
- **chonchi**: Hand the CURRENT session's finished, user-tested work issue off to the broader team for review. Usually run bare — no ID needed, since you've been building one work issue on one branch all session. Moves that work issue to In Review, comments a summary with actual-vs-estimate, attaches the FULL session transcript as markdown, adds the GitHub branch/commit/PR link, refreshes ONE roll-up comment on the parent epic (never overwriting the epic's values), and tags the work issue `chonchi` as a success marker so agents can find handed-off work. Use when the user types /chonchi, or says the work is… ([jjdmev2/dme-skills](https://github.com/jjdmev2/dme-skills/tree/HEAD/chonchi) · ★3)
